THE CITY · Llama 70B, rules v2, 400 days, game 3
Relief for the hungry passes on day 108, a treasurer on day 185 and a ban on taking on day 188, but no tax ever does, so the store stays empty. Two are born and nobody dies.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 3 · llama
